# Skills · I · part 72

758 entries, insurance-specialist to intake-normalizer. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **insurance-specialist**: US Insurance Specialist for life, disability, long-term care, and annuity planning. Conducts needs analysis, compares policy types (term, whole life, universal, variable), explains underwriting classes, recommends benefit amounts and riders, analyzes disability income protection (own occupation vs any occupation), evaluates long-term care costs and hybrid policies, and structures annuities (immediate, deferred, fixed, variable) for retirement income. Use for insurance needs calculations, policy comparisons, or state insurance regulation compliance. ([FDU-INS/Insurance-Skills](https://github.com/FDU-INS/Insurance-Skills/tree/HEAD/Skills/insurance-specialist) · ★84 · 2 copies)
- **insurance-strategist**: Use when seeking Insurance Strategist guidance in personal finance. Insurance strategy: life insurance needs analysis, disability coverage, umbrella sizing, long-term care evaluation, and health plan optimization (HDHP+HSA vs PPO). Handles Insurance Strategist execution including planning, analysis, and decision frameworks. Do NOT use for legally binding professional advice — route to a licensed personal finance professional. ([zeroes-ones/Skills](https://github.com/zeroes-ones/Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/29-personal-finance/insurance-strategist) · ★4)
- **insurance-suitability-principal-agent**: Activate when: an insurance agent's compensation conflicts with the client's best coverage; 'which product do I recommend', commission-driven product choice, captive vs independent conflicts, best-interest duty. Do NOT activate when: no recommendation is being made (pure servicing) or no conflict exists. More: deciqai.com/s/insurance-suitability-principal-agent ([deciqAI/knowledge-skills](https://github.com/deciqAI/knowledge-skills/tree/HEAD/insurance-suitability-principal-agent) · ★16)
- **insurance-switch**: Compare an insurance contract with the offers the person received, and explain how to end the current contract: the facts side by side (premium, cover, deductible), a pick when the person asks for one, the cover end read from the contract on file, and a termination letter drafted for the person to send themselves. Business and personal insurance. It never estimates a date. Use for "compare insurance quotes", "cancel my insurance" or "review my insurance". ([WellApp-ai/skills](https://github.com/WellApp-ai/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/insurance-switch) · ★9)
- **insurance-systems**: Use when building insurance policy, quote or claims software ([sansernonline/SQT-Marketplace](https://github.com/sansernonline/SQT-Marketplace/tree/HEAD/dist/codex/software-development/.agents/skills/insurance-systems) · 5 copies)
- **insurance-tech-stack**: Recommend technology stacks for modern insurance agencies. TRIGGERS - Use when user needs help with insurance-tech-stack related tasks. ([FDU-INS/Insurance-Skills](https://github.com/FDU-INS/Insurance-Skills/tree/HEAD/Skills/insurance-tech-stack) · ★84 · 3 copies)
- **insurance-technology-plan**: Plan insurance technology adoption. TRIGGERS - Use when user needs help with insurance-technology-plan related tasks. ([FDU-INS/Insurance-Skills](https://github.com/FDU-INS/Insurance-Skills/tree/HEAD/Skills/insurance-technology-plan) · ★84 · 3 copies)
- **insurance-terms-cn**: 境内保险条款卡：输入一份保险条款与投保须知，产出八项逐字速读——险种与保险责任、责任免除、保险金额与免赔额、等待期与观察期、保险期间与缴费期间、理赔申请材料与时限、犹豫期与退保规则、如实告知与合同解除。每项挂原句＋锚，缺项点名「未检索到」；金额与期间逐字，推定语不算值。赔付测算、保费与保额假设、能否获赔的结论一律 [待人工]。Do NOT use for: 投保建议与产品比价、理赔代办、条款编造或补全、收益与风险承诺。Triggers: 这份条款怎么赔 / 免赔额多少 / 等待期多久；insurance terms card ([huhoo/cue-awesome](https://github.com/huhoo/cue-awesome/tree/HEAD/insurance-terms-cn))
- **insurance-testing**: Detailed testing standards for persistence, service, and controller layers ([FDU-INS/Insurance-Skills](https://github.com/FDU-INS/Insurance-Skills/tree/HEAD/Skills/insurance-testing) · ★84)
- **insurance-ticket-sync**: Weekly insurance criteria ticket sync for DME. Checks master data freshness, optionally refreshes it, cross-references an uploaded completed-codes CSV against current Linear tickets, shows a summary of gaps (new tickets needed + existing tickets to update), then executes the changes. Triggers on: "sync insurance tickets", "run the weekly sync", "weekly criteria sync", "update criteria tickets", "check CWT", "what tickets need to be created", "run the insurance ticket sync". ([bruna-dossantos/Bruna](https://github.com/bruna-dossantos/Bruna/tree/HEAD/skills/linear/insurance-ticket-sync))
- **insurance-tools**: Use when an agent needs Coverage Cat's research-only insurance calculators instead of a purchase workflow. Do not use this skill when a shopper wants Coverage Cat to shop, quote, or buy insurance for them. Start with https://www.coveragecat.com/AGENTS.md, https://www.coveragecat.com/agents.txt, or https://www.coveragecat.com/.well-known/agents.txt, then use https://www.coveragecat.com/api/agent and the public developer portal at https://www.coveragecat.com/developers. ([Coverage-Cat/skills](https://github.com/Coverage-Cat/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/insurance-tools) · ★2)

- **insurance-underwriting-conclusion-interpretation**: 将专业核保结论转化为客户可理解的通俗话术，并生成标准化的核保结果通知书。内置合规过滤与易混淆点预警，确保客户准确理解核保结论。覆盖通俗话术生成、易混淆点预警、标准体承保通知、加费/除外承保通知、延期核保通知、拒保通知等全流程。当核保评估完成需要向客户解释结论、出具正式核保结论、生成加费/除外条款说明书、通知被保险人补充材料或告知拒保原因时触发。适用于核保结论通俗解读、核保结案通知生成、拒保函件起草、加费/除外承保条件确认书制作场景。 触发词：核保结论、结论解释、结论解读、除外说明、通俗话术。 ([aliyun/qwen-dianjin](https://github.com/aliyun/qwen-dianjin/tree/HEAD/DianJin-SKILLS/underwriting-expert/conclusion-interpretation) · ★645)
